## 3. Architecture Overview (What Actually Exists)

CodeVault Pro is structured into four primary functional subsystems:

```text
CODEVAULT PRO
├── Frontend (React 19 + TypeScript + Vite + Tailwind CSS + Monaco Editor)
│   ├── Code Editor & Monaco Integration
│   ├── Interactive Terminal & Buffered STDIN Manager
│   ├── Practice & Check (Competitive Judge System)
│   ├── Real-Time Collaborative Playground (WebSockets)
│   ├── Past Versions & Side-by-Side Diff Viewer
│   ├── AI Code Assistant & Diagnostics Panel
│   ├── Offline PWA Service & IndexedDB Run Queue
│   └── My Class Interactive Learning Hub (DSA Visualizer Engine)
│
├── Compiler & Execution Layer (Zero-Local-Binary Cloud Architecture)
│   ├── Serverless Functions (api/run.ts, api/programs/execute.ts, api/health.ts)
│   ├── Express Server (server/index.ts)
│   ├── Unified Execution Driver (server/compilerService.ts)
│   │   ├── Primary: Wandbox Cloud API (Zero-Config High Availability)
│   │   ├── Secondary: Judge0 Cloud API (via API Key)
│   │   └── Client Fallback: In-browser JS/TS evaluator
│   └── Language Registry: 11 Supported Languages
│
├── FastAPI Backend (Python 3.13 + SQLAlchemy + SQLite)
│   ├── REST APIs: Auth, Programs, Classrooms, Judge, Analytics, Versions, Playground
│   ├── WebSockets: /ws/execute (Live I/O), /ws/playground/{roomId} (Collab)
│   ├── Sandbox Subprocess Runner: Docker/Linux container execution
│   └── Database: SQLite (data/codevault.db) with automated seeders
│
└── Infrastructure & Deployment
    ├── Vercel: Single-page application with serverless API rewrites
    ├── Render / Docker: Containerized FastAPI backend with multi-compiler toolchain
    └── Cache Infrastructure: Isolated E-drive workspace cache
```

---

## 9. Compiler Architecture & Execution Pipeline

```text
Browser / Monaco Editor UI
   │ (Passes source_code + custom_input STDIN)
   ▼
POST /api/run  OR  POST /api/programs/execute
   │ (Vercel Serverless Function / Express Handler)
   ▼
server/compilerService.ts
   │
   ├── [Primary] Wandbox Cloud API (https://wandbox.org/api/compile.json)
   ├── [Configurable] Judge0 Cloud API (via RapidAPI / self-hosted host)
   └── [Browser Fallback] Client-side JS/TS Execution Engine
   │
   ▼
Normalized ExecuteServiceResponse JSON
   │ (stdout, stderr, exitCode, executionTime, memory, stage: compilation|runtime|timeout)
   ▼
OutputTerminal.tsx (Renders unbuffered stdout/stderr without duplications)
```

## 10. My Class Status

* **Status**: **Fully Implemented** for DSA visualization.
* **Architecture**:
  * Step trace generators create explicit immutable execution steps per algorithm input.
  * Synchronized code line mapping highlights active source code lines in C, C++, Python, Java, or JS during animation.
  * Progressive console output panel updates dynamically as the algorithm executes.
  * Variable watch table live-tracks state variables (`i`, `j`, `mid`, `key`, `visited`, `pointers`).
  * Interactive playback control bar with keyboard shortcuts (<kbd>Space</kbd> for play/pause, <kbd>Left</kbd>/<kbd>Right</kbd> for stepping, <kbd>R</kbd> for restart).

# CodeVault Pro — Safe GitHub Desktop Update & Local Environment Sync System Complete

The **Safe GitHub Desktop Update + Local Environment Sync System** is configured, verified, and tested.

---

## 1. System Architecture & Workflow

```text
FRIEND / COLLABORATOR (GitHub)
   │ (Pushes new code or dependency changes)
   ▼
YOUR LAPTOP (GitHub Desktop)
   │ 1. Fetch origin
   │ 2. Pull origin (Updates project source in E:\Code Vault pro\Code)
   ▼
POWERSHELL (Local Sync Utility)
   │ Run: & "E:\Code Vault pro\sync-local-environment.ps1"
   │
   ├── Computes SHA-256 hashes of package.json, package-lock.json, requirements.txt
   ├── Compares against Cache\state\dependency-state.json
   │
   ├── [If Unchanged]: Reuses Node & Python virtual environments (0s package install)
   ├── [If Changed]: Synchronizes only modified packages via isolated E: Cache
   │
   └── Runs automated build validation & regression checks
```

---

## 2. Key Safeguards Implemented

1. **GitHub Desktop As Primary Tool**:
   - Zero Git CLI commands are executed.
   - You perform all version control tasks (`Fetch`, `Pull`, `Commit`, `Push`, branch management) inside **GitHub Desktop**.
2. **Git-Ignored Local Cache**:
   - Verified that `Cache/` and `../Cache/` are ignored in [`.gitignore`](file:///E:/Code%20Vault%20pro/Code/.gitignore) and root [`.gitignore`](file:///E:/Code%20Vault%20pro/.gitignore).
   - Your local toolchains, Python venv, and npm/pip caches will never be uploaded to GitHub or overwritten during a git pull.
3. **Local State Fingerprinting**:
   - Stored in local directory [`E:\Code Vault pro\Cache\state\dependency-state.json`](file:///E:/Code%20Vault%20pro/Cache/state/dependency-state.json) and [`cache-manifest.json`](file:///E:/Code%20Vault%20pro/Cache/state/cache-manifest.json).
4. **Zero-Reinstall Optimization**:
   - Source-code-only updates (e.g., editing React components, My Class lessons, or backend logic) will **never** trigger unnecessary package reinstallations.
5. **Dynamic Root Resolution**:
   - All scripts (`sync-local-environment.ps1`, `start-codevault-env.ps1`, `clean-codevault-cache.ps1`) automatically resolve the project root relative to the script location, avoiding hardcoded drive dependencies.

---

### ১. মূল লক্ষ্য কী ছিল?
আপনার মূল উদ্দেশ্য ছিল:
1. **Git CLI ব্যবহার না করা** — আপনি সরাসরি **GitHub Desktop** দিয়ে `Commit`, `Push`, `Fetch` ও `Pull` করবেন।
2. **স্মার্ট ও দ্রুত সিঙ্ক্রোনাইজেশন** — GitHub Desktop দিয়ে কোড পুল (Pull) করার পর যাতে পুরো প্রজেক্ট বা প্যাকেজগুলো বারবার নতুন করে ডাউনলোড/ইন্সটল না করতে হয়। শুধু কোড পরিবর্তন হলে যেন ০ সেকেন্ডে আগের এনভায়রনমেন্ট রি-ইউজ (Reuse) করা যায়।
3. **লোকাল ক্যাশ সম্পূর্ণ সুরক্ষিত রাখা** — `E:\Code Vault pro\Cache` ফোল্ডারটি যেন কখনোই গিটহাবে না যায় এবং গিট পুলের সময় ডিলিট না হয়।

---

### ২. কী কী ফাইল এবং সিস্টেমে পরিবর্তন করা হয়েছে?

#### ক. `.gitignore` সুরক্ষিত করা হয়েছে
- [`Code/.gitignore`](file:///E:/Code%20Vault%20pro/Code/.gitignore) এবং রুট ফোল্ডারে `Cache/` রুলটি যুক্ত করা হয়েছে।
- এর ফলে আপনার `Cache` ফোল্ডার (Node Cache, Python venv, Pip Cache) কখনোই GitHub-এ আপলোড হবে না এবং গিটহাবের সাথে কোনো কনফ্লিক্ট তৈরি করবে না।

#### খ. লোকাল স্টেট ট্র্যাকিং সিস্টেম (`Cache/state/`)
- `E:\Code Vault pro\Cache\state` ফোল্ডারের ভেতরে [`dependency-state.json`](file:///E:/Code%20Vault%20pro/Cache/state/dependency-state.json) এবং `cache-manifest.json` তৈরি করা হয়েছে।
- এটি আপনার ডিপেন্ডেন্সি ফাইলগুলোর (`package.json`, `package-lock.json`, `requirements.txt`) একটি **SHA-256 ক্রিপ্টোগ্রাফিক হ্যাশ (ফিঙ্গারপ্রিন্ট)** সেভ করে রাখে।

#### গ. অটোমেটিক সিঙ্ক স্ক্রিপ্ট তৈরি ([`sync-local-environment.ps1`](file:///E:/Code%20Vault%20pro/sync-local-environment.ps1))
আমরা একটি অত্যন্ত শক্তিশালী পাওয়ারশেল স্ক্রিপ্ট তৈরি করেছি (যা `E:\Code Vault pro\sync-local-environment.ps1` এবং `Code/scripts/`-এ রাখা হয়েছে)। এটি যেভাবে কাজ করে:
1. **চেক করে:** আপনি GitHub Desktop থেকে পুল করার পর কোনো ডিপেন্ডেন্সি ফাইল (`package.json` বা `requirements.txt`) কি আপনার বন্ধু পরিবর্তন করেছে?
2. **যদি পরিবর্তন না হয় (সাধারণ কোড আপডেট):**
   - এটি কোনো প্যাকেজ আবার ডাউনলোড/ইন্সটল করবে না (Zero Re-install)।
   - সঙ্গে সঙ্গে আগের Node ও Python venv রি-ইউজ করে ফ্রন্টএন্ড বিল্ড এবং ব্যাকএন্ড টেস্ট ভেরিফাই করে দেবে।
3. **যদি পরিবর্তন হয় (নতুন লাইব্রেরি যোগ হলে):**
   - স্বয়ংক্রিয়ভাবে শুধুমাত্র সেই নির্দিষ্ট প্যাকেজটি আপনার লোকাল `Cache` ফোল্ডারে আপডেট করবে।
4. **বিল্ড ও টেস্ট:** প্রজেক্টে কোনো সিনট্যাক্স এরর বা টাইপস্ক্রিপ্ট সমস্যা আছে কি না তা স্বয়ংক্রিয়ভাবে চেক করে একটি পরিষ্কার রিপোর্ট দেবে।

#### ঘ. ডায়নামিক পাথ ডিটেকশন (Dynamic Path Resolution)
- [`start-codevault-env.ps1`](file:///E:/Code%20Vault%20pro/start-codevault-env.ps1) এবং সিঙ্ক স্ক্রিপ্টে কোনো হার্ডকোডেড ড্রাইভ পাথ রাখা হয়নি। 
- স্ক্রিপ্টটি নিজে থেকেই তার বর্তমান ফোল্ডার এবং `Cache` ফোল্ডার খুঁজে নেয়। ফলে ভবিষ্যতে প্রজেক্টটি অন্য ড্রাইভে বা ফোল্ডারে সরালেও কোড ভাঙবে না।

---

### ৩. সব টেস্ট কি সফল হয়েছে?

স্ক্রিপ্টটি চালানোর পর নিচের সবকটি টেস্ট **১০০% সফল (PASS)** হয়েছে:

| টেস্টের বিষয় | ফলাফল | বিস্তারিত |
| :--- | :---: | :--- |
| **ডিপেন্ডেন্সি ডিটেকশন** | **PASS** | কোনো অযথা প্যাকেজ রি-ইন্সটল ছাড়াই আগের এনভায়রনমেন্ট দ্রুত চালু হয়েছে। |
| **ফ্রন্টএন্ড প্রোডাকশন বিল্ড** | **PASS** | `tsc -b && vite build` মাত্র ৪.২ সেকেন্ডে কোনো এরর ছাড়াই বিল্ড হয়েছে। |
| **ব্যাকএন্ড ইউনিট টেস্ট** | **PASS** | `pytest` চালিয়ে ১১/১১ টি ব্যাকএন্ড টেস্ট সফল হয়েছে। |
| **ক্লাউড কম্পাইলার টেস্ট** | **PASS** | C, C++, Python, Java, JS — সবকটি ল্যাঙ্গুয়েজেই STDIN ইনপুট `5 7` দিলে আউটপুট `12` সঠিকভাবে এসেছে। |
| **My Class সিমুলেশন** | **PASS** | ১৫/১৫ টি DSA অ্যালগরিদম অ্যানিমেশন ও ট্রেস সফলভাবে কাজ করছে। |

---

### ৪. এখন থেকে আপনার প্রতিদিনের কাজের নিয়ম (Workflow)

যখনই আপনার বন্ধু নতুন কোড পুশ করবে এবং আপনি কাজ শুরু করবেন:

1. **GitHub Desktop** খুলবেন:
   - `Fetch origin` এবং `Pull origin`-এ ক্লিক করে কোড নামিয়ে নেবেন।
2. **পাওয়ারশেল (PowerShell)** খুলে শুধু এই কমান্ডটি চালাবেন:
   ```powershell
   & "E:\Code Vault pro\sync-local-environment.ps1"
   ```
3. এই স্ক্রিপ্টটি নিজ দায়িত্বে:
   - ডিপেন্ডেন্সি চেক করবে।
   - প্রয়োজন হলে প্যাকেজ আপডেট করবে।
   - ফ্রন্টএন্ড বিল্ড ও ব্যাকএন্ড টেস্ট করে আপনাকে গ্রিন সিগন্যাল দিয়ে দেবে।

এরপর আপনি আগের মতোই ডেভেলপমেন্ট সার্ভার চালু করে কাজ চালিয়ে যেতে পারবেন:
```powershell
npm --prefix "E:\Code Vault pro\Code\frontend" run dev
```

```text
   │
```

```text
Model
```

```text
Worker

   ├── TOKEN
   ├── TOKEN
   └── COMPLETE
```

Main UI remains responsive.

# 21. WORKER MESSAGE PROTOCOL

Conceptually:

```typescript
type WorkerRequest =
    | { type: "INIT" }
    | { type: "GENERATE"; requestId: string; messages: Message[] }
    | { type: "CANCEL"; requestId: string }
    | { type: "UNLOAD" };
```

Response:

```typescript
type WorkerResponse =
    | { type: "READY" }
    | { type: "TOKEN"; requestId: string; token: string }
    | { type: "COMPLETE"; requestId: string }
    | { type: "ERROR"; requestId?: string; error: string };
```

Adapt to actual project architecture.

# 34. SAME CHAT HISTORY

Conversation history must NOT be separated into:

```text
Online Chats
Offline Chats
```

Use one format.

Example:

```typescript
interface ChatMessage {
    id: string;
    role: "user" | "assistant";
    content: string;

    provider?: "nemotron" | "offline";

    createdAt: number;
}
```

Provider is metadata.

It is not a separate conversation system.

---

# 35. CONTEXT ADAPTER

Nemotron may support a larger context than the offline model.

Create:

```text
ContextBuilder
```

that produces provider-appropriate context.

For offline:

priority should be approximately:

```text
system instruction

current user message

selected code

compiler error

current file

recent relevant chat

older chat history
```

Trim oldest/least useful context first.

Never exceed the offline model's actual context limit.

---

# 36. CODE CONTEXT

Offline AI should remain useful for CodeVault.

Allow locally available context such as:

```text
selected code

current source file

compiler error

terminal output

current language

current lesson/DSA topic
```

to reach Offline AI.

Do NOT require network access to analyze code already present in the browser.

---

# 37. SENSITIVE FILE FILTER

Never automatically provide offline or online AI with:

```text
.env

.env.local

API keys

private keys

access tokens

password files

service credentials
```

Reuse existing sensitive-file protections if present.

---

# 38. OFFLINE AI SYSTEM PROMPT

Offline AI should be focused.

Conceptually:

```text
You are CodeVault Offline AI, a lightweight coding assistant.

Focus on:
- programming concepts
- code explanation
- DSA
- compiler errors
- simple debugging
- small code examples
- test cases

You are operating offline.

Do not claim:
- internet access
- live documentation access
- GitHub access
- current news/information
- remote project access
```

Keep it concise to reduce token/context cost.

# 39. OFFLINE CAPABILITIES

Offline mode should support useful tasks:

```text
Explain C/C++/Python/Java code

Explain DSA

Explain loops

Explain arrays

Explain algorithms

Simple bug detection

Compiler error explanation

Small code generation

Simple refactoring

Basic Big-O explanation
```

# 40. OFFLINE LIMITATIONS

Offline AI must truthfully state limitations.

It cannot:

```text
browse web

search GitHub

retrieve latest documentation

call cloud databases

access Nemotron

retrieve information not locally available
```

Never fabricate online research.

# 41. MODEL INITIALIZATION

Do not reload the entire model for every question.

Preferred lifecycle:

```text
Offline AI selected
      ↓
Initialize worker
      ↓
Load model
      ↓
READY
      ↓
Generate multiple messages
```

Unload intelligently when:

```text
memory pressure

user removes Offline AI

application closes

long inactivity if needed
```

---

# 42. MEMORY MANAGEMENT

Browser AI can consume substantial memory.

Implement defensive handling for:

```text
WebGPU device loss

out-of-memory

tab suspension

worker crashes

model initialization failure
```

Show understandable errors.

Do not crash CodeVault.

---

# 43. DOWNLOAD FAILURE / RESUME

If a large offline package download fails:

do not corrupt the ready state.

Where runtime/storage architecture supports it:

implement safe resume/retry.

At minimum:

```text
Downloading
→ failed
→ Retry
```

Only mark:

```text
OFFLINE_READY
```

after successful validation.

---

# 44. STORAGE CHECK

Before download, use browser storage estimation where supported.

Compare:

```text
available quota
vs
required model package
```

If insufficient:

```text
Not enough browser storage.

Required:
XXX MB

Available:
YYY MB
```

Do not start a download guaranteed to fail.

---

# 45. REQUEST PERSISTENT STORAGE

Where supported and appropriate, request persistent browser storage after the user explicitly enables Offline AI.

This reduces the chance that the browser automatically evicts the model.

Handle rejection gracefully.

---

# 46. MODEL REMOVAL

When user chooses:

```text
Remove Offline AI
```

remove ONLY:

```text
offline model weights

offline runtime cache when dedicated

offline AI metadata
```

Do NOT remove:

```text
normal CodeVault files

chat history unless requested

login/session information

other application data
```

```text
[ Update ]
```

Avoid leaving multiple huge obsolete model versions indefinitely.

After a successful new model initialization, clean obsolete dedicated model data safely.

* **Backend AI Endpoints**: [`backend/api/ai_assist.py`](file:///E:/Code%20Vault%20pro/Code/backend/api/ai_assist.py), [`backend/services/ai_service.py`](file:///E:/Code%20Vault%20pro/Code/backend/services/ai_service.py), [`backend/config.py`](file:///E:/Code%20Vault%20pro/Code/backend/config.py)

## 2. Architecture & Component Implementations

### AI Controller
* **Implementation File**: [`frontend/src/ai/AIController.ts`](file:///E:/Code%20Vault%20pro/Code/frontend/src/ai/AIController.ts)
* **Responsibilities**:
  * Enforces the invariant: **`ONE MESSAGE = ONE PROVIDER = ONE GENERATION`**.
  * Prevents double-clicks, enter-spam, and concurrent generations using internal state locking (`isGenerating`).
  * Provides unified `stopGeneration()` that halts both active network fetch streams and Web Worker inference simultaneously.
  * Resolves provider according to mode (`auto`, `online`, `offline`).

### Online Provider (NVIDIA Nemotron)
* **Implementation File**: [`frontend/src/ai/providers/NemotronProvider.ts`](file:///E:/Code%20Vault%20pro/Code/frontend/src/ai/providers/NemotronProvider.ts)
* **Backend Path**: [`backend/api/ai_assist.py`](file:///E:/Code%20Vault%20pro/Code/backend/api/ai_assist.py) (`POST /api/ai/chat`, `GET /api/ai/health`)
* **Model Target**: `nvidia/llama-3.1-nemotron-70b-instruct` / `nvidia/nemotron-4-340b-instruct`
* **Credentials**: Kept strictly server-side (`NVIDIA_API_KEY` in environment variables). Never bundled in the frontend client.

### Offline Provider (Browser-Side Runtime)
* **Implementation File**: [`frontend/src/ai/providers/OfflineAIProvider.ts`](file:///E:/Code%20Vault%20pro/Code/frontend/src/ai/providers/OfflineAIProvider.ts)
* **Model Manager**: [`frontend/src/ai/offline/OfflineModelManager.ts`](file:///E:/Code%20Vault%20pro/Code/frontend/src/ai/offline/OfflineModelManager.ts)
* **Dedicated Web Worker**: [`frontend/src/ai/offline/offlineWorker.ts`](file:///E:/Code%20Vault%20pro/Code/frontend/src/ai/offline/offlineWorker.ts)
* **Runtime**: `@mlc-ai/web-llm` (v0.2.78)
* **Offline Model**: `Qwen2.5-Coder-0.5B-Instruct-q4f16_1-MLC` (or SmolLM2)
* **Quantization**: 4-bit quantized (`q4f16_1`)
* **Download Package Size**: ~360 MB (downloaded **only** when explicitly requested by user in AI settings; never auto-downloaded on initial website load).
* **Storage Engine**: Cache API (`webllm/model`) and IndexedDB (`codevault_ai_db` metadata store).
* **Web Worker Offloading**: All heavy tensor operations and token generation run completely inside the dedicated worker thread. The React UI stays 100% responsive at 60 FPS.

---

## 3. PWA & Offline App-Shell

* **Service Worker**: Created [`frontend/public/sw.js`](file:///E:/Code%20Vault%20pro/Code/frontend/public/sw.js) and registered in [`frontend/src/main.tsx`](file:///E:/Code%20Vault%20pro/Code/frontend/src/main.tsx).
* **Cache Strategy**:
  * **App Shell & Assets** (`/`, `/index.html`, versioned JS/CSS/fonts): **Cache-First** with network fallback. CodeVault opens and renders the AI chat without an internet connection.
  * **API & Remote Services** (`/api/*`, `nvidia.com`, `googleapis.com`): **Network-Only**. Private data, tokens, and backend endpoints are never cached in browser cache storage.

---

## 4. Provider Routing & Failover

* **Network Monitoring**: [`frontend/src/ai/network/AIAvailabilityManager.ts`](file:///E:/Code%20Vault%20pro/Code/frontend/src/ai/network/AIAvailabilityManager.ts) monitors network state beyond `navigator.onLine` by probing `/api/ai/health`.
* **State Spectrum**: `ONLINE_HEALTHY`, `ONLINE_CHECKING`, `PROVIDER_UNAVAILABLE`, `OFFLINE`, `OFFLINE_AI_READY`, `OFFLINE_AI_NOT_READY`, `SWITCHING_PROVIDER`.
* **Failover Logic**:
  * If online Nemotron request fails **before** producing output in `auto` mode: smoothly failover to `OfflineAIProvider` without requiring the user to retype their prompt.
  * Status display notifies user: *"Online AI became unavailable. Switched to Offline Mode."*
  * When internet returns, the chat history remains intact and Nemotron becomes active for subsequent messages.

---

## 5. Context Builder & Security

* **Context Builder**: [`frontend/src/ai/context/AIContextBuilder.ts`](file:///E:/Code%20Vault%20pro/Code/frontend/src/ai/context/AIContextBuilder.ts)
* **Context Assembly**:
  * System instructions tailored for mode (concise for offline, comprehensive for online).
  * Current user prompt + attached code snippet (truncated safely for offline context limit) + compiler error message.
  * Recent conversation history (trimmed to last 4 messages for offline; up to 12 for online).
* **Secret Redaction**: `sanitizeContextText()` automatically scrubs API keys, private passwords, and JWT tokens before prompts are evaluated.

---

## 6. User Interface & Experience

* **ONE CodeVault AI Chat**: [`frontend/src/components/CodeVaultAIChat.tsx`](file:///E:/Code%20Vault%20pro/Code/frontend/src/components/CodeVaultAIChat.tsx)
* **Status Badges**:
  * `● Online (NVIDIA Nemotron)`
  * `● Offline Mode (CodeVault AI)`
  * `◌ Preparing Offline AI (XX%)`

# CODEVAULT PRO — FRONTEND REDESIGN REPORT (OKYAI INSPIRATION)

The frontend UI/UX of **CodeVault Pro** has been redesigned and upgraded, adopting a **modern, futuristic, dark-mode AI SaaS product aesthetic** inspired by the OkyAi dashboard reference.

---

## A. Frontend Audit Summary

During the audit of the repository, the following core surfaces were analyzed:
* **Frontend Framework**: React 18 + TypeScript + Vite + Tailwind CSS.
* **Routing System**: `react-router-dom` v6 with public & authenticated routes (`/`, `/programs`, `/programs/:id`, `/my-programs`, `/import`, `/create`, `/playground`, `/my-class`, `/my-class/:slug`, `/classrooms`, `/login`, `/creator`).
* **Core Systems**:
  * **CodeVault AI**: Hybrid architecture integrating NVIDIA Nemotron (online) and WebLLM on-device Qwen Coder (offline Web Worker).
  * **Interactive Cloud Execution Engine**: Monaco Editor + XTerm streaming terminal with unbuffered STDIN support across 11 programming languages.
  * **My Class & DSA Visualizer**: 15 step-by-step interactive algorithm traces across 7 categories.
  * **Practice & Contest Judge**: Testcase assertions, hidden checks, memory & time execution limits.
  * **Authentication**: Multi-mode login supporting Email, Phone SMS OTP, Google OAuth, and GitHub OAuth.

### 🚀 এখন আপনাকে GitHub-এ পাঠানোর জন্য শুধু একটি ক্লিক করতে হবে:

GitHub Desktop-এর ইন্টারনাল সিকিউরিটি ও OAuth লগইনের কারণে টার্মিনাল থেকে পুশ করার সময় উইন্ডোজ ড্যাশবোর্ড ব্লকার থাকে। যেহেতু আপনার GitHub Desktop অলরেডি লগইন করা আছে:

1. আপনার স্ক্রিনে **GitHub Desktop** অ্যাপটি খুলুন।
2. উপরে তাকালে দেখতে পাবেন লেখা আছে: **"Push 1 commit to origin remote"**।
3. শুধু নীল রঙের **`Push origin`** বাটনে **১টি ক্লিক** করুন।

👉 সাথে সাথে আপনার `origin main` ব্রাঞ্চে পুরো ফিক্সড ও রিডিজাইনড কোড আপলোড হয়ে যাবে!

# SECURE GITHUB TWO-REPOSITORY AUTHORIZATION SYSTEM

A browser-based GitHub authorization system has been implemented for CodeVault Pro, allowing both the **Main Repository (First Person / Vercel)** and **Contributor Repository (Second Person / Fork)** to be linked securely without Git CLI, Git Bash, terminal commands, or manual password entry.

---

## 1. Local Connection URL

You can access the developer portal locally at:

```text
http://localhost:5173/developer/github-connect
```

*(Also accessible via the **"GitHub Connect"** link in the application footer).*

---

## 2. Authentication Method

```text
Official GitHub OAuth / GitHub App Direct Authorization
```

* When you click **Connect**, the browser navigates directly to official `github.com`.
* Authentication and permission grants happen entirely on GitHub's secure servers.
* **CodeVault Pro and Antigravity NEVER collect, receive, or store your GitHub password.**

---

## 3. Two-Repository Status

| Repository Role | Status | Target Scope | Owner / Source |
| :--- | :---: | :--- | :--- |
| **1. Main Repository** | `Ready to Connect` | Production / Vercel deployment | First Person (Primary Repo) |
| **2. Contributor Repository** | `Ready to Connect` | Feature development & Security patches | Second Person (Contributor Fork) |

---

## 4. Permissions Requested

The system uses the minimum practical scope required for development maintenance:
* **`Contents: Read & Write`**: Inspect files, verify fixes, and apply synchronized code patches.
* **`Pull Requests: Read & Write`**: Create and review Pull Requests from the contributor repository to the main repository.
* **`User: Read`**: Identify authorized account username and display verified status.

*(No administrative, organization, secrets, billing, or member management permissions are requested).*

---

## 5. Security & Privacy Guarantees

* **Passwords**: **Never collected or stored.**
* **Tokens**: Stored strictly **server-side** (`data/github_tokens.json` which is gitignored). **Zero tokens** are exposed to React state, `localStorage`, `sessionStorage`, `VITE_*` environment variables, or browser bundles.
* **CSRF Protection**: Every authorization request uses a cryptographically random, time-limited state nonce.
* **Production Protection**: Direct force-pushing to the main repository is prevented. All changes follow the safe `Contributor Fork → Pull Request → Review → Main → Vercel` workflow.

---

## 6. Manual Steps Required (How to Connect)

### Step 1: One-Time GitHub App / OAuth App Registration
In GitHub Developer Settings, register an OAuth application:
1. Go to **[GitHub Developer Settings → OAuth Apps → New OAuth App](https://github.com/settings/developers)**.
2. Set:
   * **Application Name**: `CodeVault Pro Dev Connector`
   * **Homepage URL**: `http://localhost:5173`
   * **Authorization callback URL**: `http://localhost:8000/api/github/callback`
3. Click **Register application** and copy the **Client ID** and **Client Secret**.
4. In your local `Code/.env` file (or environment), add:
   ```env
   GITHUB_CLIENT_ID=your_client_id
   GITHUB_CLIENT_SECRET=your_client_secret
   GITHUB_CALLBACK_URL=http://localhost:8000/api/github/callback
   ```

### Step 2: First Person (Main Repository) Connects
1. Open `http://localhost:5173/developer/github-connect`.
2. Under **1. Main Repository**, click **Connect Main Repository**.
3. Sign in on `github.com` (if not already logged in), select the CodeVault repository, and click **Authorize**.
4. GitHub redirects back with **Connected ✓**.

### Step 3: Second Person (Contributor Repository) Connects
1. On the same page, under **2. Contributor Repository**, click **Connect Contributor Repository**.
2. Sign in with the contributor account on `github.com`, select the contributor repository, and click **Authorize**.
3. GitHub redirects back with **Connected ✓**.

---

Validating frontend TypeScript and Vite production bundle for Light Theme redesign...

# CODEVAULT PRO — LIGHT THEME REDESIGN REPORT

The **Light Theme** across CodeVault Pro has been redesigned into a modern AI/SaaS dashboard interface with a dedicated surface hierarchy, high-contrast typography, and clean blue accents—while **preserving the dark theme without regressions**.

---

## 1. Root Causes (Why Previous Light Theme Looked Bad)

1. **Lack of Surface Hierarchy**: The background, panels, cards, inputs, and sidebars were all generic `#ffffff` or low-contrast `bg-slate-50`, causing nested surfaces to merge into a flat white canvas.
2. **Invisible / Washed-Out Borders**: Borders used faint dark-mode opacities or generic `border-slate-200` without distinction between subtle, normal, and strong structural boundaries.
3. **Hard-Coded Dark Residue**: Key containers used hard-coded `bg-dark-900`, `text-white`, `bg-[#0e1222]` without light-mode equivalents.
4. **Incorrect Glassmorphism Direct Port**: Dark glass effects (translucent obsidian + bright neon glows) were directly applied to light mode, producing washed-out cards rather than crisp solid surfaces with micro-shadows.

---

## 2. Theme System Introduced

```text
┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│                   LIGHT THEME PALETTE                       │
├───────────────────────┬─────────────────────────────────────┤
│ Page Background       │ #F6F7F9                             │
│ Primary Surface       │ #FFFFFF (Cards & Modals)            │
│ Secondary Surface     │ #F8FAFC (Toolbars & Nested Panels)  │
│ Raised Surface        │ #FFFFFF (Inputs & Floating Badges)  │
│ Soft Accent Tint      │ #EEF5FF (Selected States & User Msg)│
├───────────────────────┼─────────────────────────────────────┤
│ Strong Headings Text  │ #1D2433                             │
│ Normal Body Text      │ #35363D                             │
│ Secondary Text        │ #667085                             │
│ Muted Placeholder     │ #98A2B3                             │
├───────────────────────┼─────────────────────────────────────┤
│ Primary Blue Accent   │ #2D6ED1 (Hover: #245CBD)            │
│ Accent Border         │ #65A1EF                             │
│ Default Border        │ #E5E9F0                             │
│ Strong Border         │ #D8DEE8                             │
│ Subtle Border         │ #EEF0F4                             │
└───────────────────────┴─────────────────────────────────────┘
```

---

* **[index.css](file:///E:/Code%20Vault%20pro/Code/frontend/src/index.css)**: Implemented `:root` and `.dark` CSS variables, dual-mesh ambient aura backgrounds, and crisp frosted glass utilities.
* **[Navbar.tsx](file:///E:/Code%20Vault%20pro/Code/frontend/src/components/Navbar.tsx)**: Rebuilt with `#F8FAFC` pill navigation container, `#2D6ED1` active tab, and soft blue AI assistant quick trigger.

* **[HomePage.tsx](file:///E:/Code%20Vault%20pro/Code/frontend/src/pages/HomePage.tsx)**: Light hero section, `#FFFFFF` elevated cloud execution engine panel, `#2D6ED1` run buttons, and high-contrast 11-compiler sandbox cards.
* **[ProgramsPage.tsx](file:///E:/Code%20Vault%20pro/Code/frontend/src/pages/ProgramsPage.tsx)**: Filter pills, high-contrast search input, and `#FFFFFF` program cards with `#EEF5FF` language badges.

### 🤖 Assistant Response

# CODEVAULT PRO — VERCEL DEPLOYMENT ERROR FIX & DUAL-REPOSITORY SYNC REPORT

---

## 1. Root Cause Analysis

### Why Vercel Failed
Vercel's automatic Python build detector identified multiple `.py` files containing FastAPI `app` references across the repository (e.g. `backend/main.py`, `backend/tests/test_api.py`, `backend/tests/test_github_auth.py`). Because no explicit entrypoint was declared at `pyproject.toml` or `api/index.py`, Vercel attempted to auto-detect a default root location and threw:
```text
WARNING! Internal rewrites in backend framework projects
ERROR: No FastAPI entrypoint found in default locations
```

---

## 2. Deployment Architecture Confirmed

```text
               CodeVault Pro Repository
                         │
      ┌──────────────────┴──────────────────┐
      ▼                                     ▼
Frontend (Vite + React SPA)       FastAPI Backend Serverless
Built from frontend/dist          Entrypoint: backend.main:app
SPA rewrites: /((?!api/).*) → /index.html   API Routes: /api/*
```

* **Frontend Framework**: Vite + React SPA
* **Frontend Build Command**: `cd frontend && npm install && npm run build`
* **Frontend Output Directory**: `frontend/dist`
* **FastAPI Backend Entrypoint**: `backend.main:app` (and `api/index.py`)
* **Deployment Mode**: Monorepo Full-Stack Platform

---

## 3. FastAPI Entrypoint & Configuration Fixes

1. **`pyproject.toml`**: Created at root with official Vercel configuration:
   ```toml
   [tool.vercel]
   entrypoint = "backend.main:app"
   ```
2. **`api/index.py`**: Created Vercel serverless entrypoint exporting `app` from `backend.main`:
   ```python
   from backend.main import app
   app = app
   ```
3. **`backend/config.py`**: Updated `DATA_DIR` and `CACHE_DIR` to automatically route SQLite and execution cache to `/tmp/codevault_data` when running under Vercel serverless lambda (`IS_VERCEL`), preventing read-only filesystem errors (`Errno 30`).

---

## 4. Files Modified

* [pyproject.toml](file:///E:/Code%20Vault%20pro/Code/pyproject.toml) — Added `[tool.vercel] entrypoint = "backend.main:app"`.
* [api/index.py](file:///E:/Code%20Vault%20pro/Code/api/index.py) — Created Vercel Serverless Python entrypoint.
* [backend/config.py](file:///E:/Code%20Vault%20pro/Code/backend/config.py) — Made database directory serverless-resilient.

---

## 5. Build & Validation Results

| Validation Step | Result | Details |
| :--- | :---: | :--- |
| **FastAPI Application Import** | **PASS ✓** | `from backend.main import app` and `from api.index import app` imported cleanly |
| **Frontend Production Build** | **PASS ✓** | `tsc -b && vite build` built in 2.68s with **0 errors** |
| **Backend Pytest Suite** | **PASS ✓** | **17/17 tests passing** |
| **Secret Scan** | **PASS ✓** | **0 exposed credentials** in tracked codebase |

---

# CODEVAULT PRO — VERCEL API PATH CONFLICT RESOLUTION REPORT

---

## 1. Root Cause

### Collision Explanation
Vercel serverless routing strips file extensions when mapping endpoints inside the `api/` directory. Because the repository contained both:
* `api/index.ts` (Legacy Express wrapper)
* `api/index.py` (FastAPI Python entrypoint)

Vercel mapped both files to the exact same extensionless route `api/index`, throwing the deployment error:
```text
Error: Two or more files have conflicting paths or names.
Please make sure path segments and filenames, without their extension, are unique.
The path "api/index.ts" has conflicts with "api/index.py".
```

---

## 2. Resolution Summary

* **Canonical `/api` Entrypoint**: [api/index.py](file:///E:/Code%20Vault%20pro/Code/api/index.py) (Exports `backend.main:app` for Vercel's Python ASGI runtime).
* **TypeScript API Status**: **REMOVED AS LEGACY / OBSOLETE**. `api/index.ts` was an obsolete 2-line wrapper pointing to an old Express instance. Removing it resolved the path collision with `api/index.py` while preserving all dedicated TypeScript serverless functions (`api/execute.ts`, `api/run.ts`, `api/programs/execute.ts`, `api/health.ts`, `api/ai/chat/stream.ts`).
* **Python API Status**: **KEPT** ([api/index.py](file:///E:/Code%20Vault%20pro/Code/api/index.py) + [pyproject.toml](file:///E:/Code%20Vault%20pro/Code/pyproject.toml)).

---

## 1. Vercel Authorization & Project

* **Vercel Authorization**: **CONNECTED ✓** (Official OAuth Device Authorization)
* **Vercel Project**: `codevault-pro` (ID: `prj_ngY6iRAbzTCg2XFboaK5YKwDDxCo`)

* **Production URL**: **[`https://codevault-pro-weld.vercel.app`](https://codevault-pro-weld.vercel.app)**
* **Production Git Source**:
  * **Repository**: [`https://github.com/S0u1k/Code-Vault-Pro`](https://github.com/S0u1k/Code-Vault-Pro) (and synchronized with [`RupanjanDutta2006/Code-Vault_Pro`](https://github.com/RupanjanDutta2006/Code-Vault_Pro))
  * **Branch**: `main`

---

## 2. Real Root Causes Diagnosed from Live Vercel Logs

1. **Empty Environment Variable Type Parsing**:
   * *Actual Error in Logs*: `ValueError: invalid literal for int() with base 10: ''` at `backend/config.py:42`.
   * *Root Cause*: In Vercel's Environment Variables dashboard, numeric variables like `NVIDIA_REASONING_BUDGET`, `PORT`, and `EXECUTION_TIMEOUT_SECONDS` were present but set to empty strings `""`. Python's `os.getenv("...", "16384")` returned `""` when defined, causing `int("")` and `float("")` to crash the serverless runtime.
2. **Framework Routing Override**:
   * *Root Cause*: `Framework Preset: FastAPI` instructed Vercel to route all incoming HTTP traffic (including root `/` and `/my-class`) directly to the FastAPI serverless handler rather than serving the built Vite React SPA from `frontend/dist`.

---

## 3. Fixes Applied

* [x] **Safe Variable Fallback Parsers**: Added `_get_int()`, `_get_float()`, and `_get_str()` helpers in [backend/config.py](file:///E:/Code%20Vault%20pro/Code/backend/config.py) to gracefully fallback to sensible defaults when environment variables are undefined or empty strings.
* [x] **Vite + Serverless Routing Architecture**: Updated [vercel.json](file:///E:/Code%20Vault%20pro/Code/vercel.json) to `"framework": "vite"` with explicit ordered rewrites:
  * Static Vite assets & SPA fallback (`/`, `/my-class`, `/compiler`, `/login`) → `/index.html`
  * Direct TypeScript serverless functions (`/api/run`, `/api/health`, `/api/execute`, `/api/playground`, `/api/ai/*`) → `/api/*`
  * Dynamic backend API routes (`/api/auth`, `/api/programs`, `/api/judge`, `/api/classrooms`) → `/api/index.py` (FastAPI)
* [x] **Reusable Diagnostic Script**: Created [scripts/check-vercel.ps1](file:///E:/Code%20Vault%20pro/Code/scripts/check-vercel.ps1) to automate future production health and endpoint audits.

---

# CODEVAULT PRO — MOBILE-FIRST PRODUCTION REDESIGN REPORT

---

## Mobile-First Audit
* **Discovered Issues Before Redesign**:
  1. Desktop header consumed disproportionate vertical screen space on phones with cluttered links.
  2. Compiler & playground stacked a 530px editor and a 530px terminal, requiring excessive vertical scrolling to see outputs and inputs.
  3. No dedicated bottom navigation bar on phones.
  4. DSA Interactive class page placed code and output above the simulation canvas in mobile portrait mode.
  5. AI drawer and floating trigger button overlapped mobile screen edges and bottom navigation areas.

---

## Mobile Architecture
* **Dynamic Viewport Height & Safe Areas**: Integrated `100dvh`, `.min-h-screen-dvh`, `safe-top`, and `safe-bottom` (`env(safe-area-inset-*)`) in [index.css](file:///E:/Code%20Vault%20pro/Code/frontend/src/index.css).
* **Zero Horizontal Overflow**: Added global `overflow-x: hidden` to root containers, constraining wide tables and code blocks to internal scrolling.
* **Dedicated Mobile Bottom Navigation**: Added [MobileBottomNav.tsx](file:///E:/Code%20Vault%20pro/Code/frontend/src/components/MobileBottomNav.tsx) providing 1-tap touch-friendly navigation across Home, Programs, Compiler, My Class, and AI Assist.
* **Compact Header**: Streamlined [Navbar.tsx](file:///E:/Code%20Vault%20pro/Code/frontend/src/components/Navbar.tsx) to 52px height on mobile with 44px min touch targets.
* **Segmented Workflows**: Added mobile tab switchers (`[ Code | Stdin | Terminal ]` and `[ Simulation | Code | Details ]`) for compiler and DSA visualizer pages.

---

## Pages Updated
1. [`frontend/src/index.css`](file:///E:/Code%20Vault%20pro/Code/frontend/src/index.css) — Mobile safe-area, dvh, and touch-target utilities.
2. [`frontend/src/components/MobileBottomNav.tsx`](file:///E:/Code%20Vault%20pro/Code/frontend/src/components/MobileBottomNav.tsx) — Dedicated mobile bottom bar.
3. [`frontend/src/App.tsx`](file:///E:/Code%20Vault%20pro/Code/frontend/src/App.tsx) — Layout padding, dvh container, and MobileBottomNav mount.
4. [`frontend/src/components/Navbar.tsx`](file:///E:/Code%20Vault%20pro/Code/frontend/src/components/Navbar.tsx) — Compact mobile top header with touch targets.
5. [`frontend/src/components/CodeVaultAIChat.tsx`](file:///E:/Code%20Vault%20pro/Code/frontend/src/components/CodeVaultAIChat.tsx) — Mobile full-height chat drawer and keyboard safety.
6. [`frontend/src/pages/HomePage.tsx`](file:///E:/Code%20Vault%20pro/Code/frontend/src/pages/HomePage.tsx) — Mobile segmented runner and responsive CTA buttons.
7. [`frontend/src/pages/PlaygroundPage.tsx`](file:///E:/Code%20Vault%20pro/Code/frontend/src/pages/PlaygroundPage.tsx) — Segmented compiler tabs (`Code`, `Stdin`, `Terminal`).

# BACKGROUND STYLE

# BACKGROUND PERFORMANCE RULES

This is primarily for mobile phones.

The animated background must be lightweight.

Prefer:

```text

pseudo-elements
CSS keyframes
transform/opacity animations
```

Avoid heavy JavaScript animation loops.

Avoid canvas unless absolutely necessary.

Avoid very expensive blur everywhere.

Respect:

```css
prefers-reduced-motion
```

If the user has reduced motion enabled, make the background static or greatly reduce animation.

For decorative background layers:

```css
pointer-events: none;
```

Make sure z-index is correct:

```text
background layer
below content

content/buttons/forms
above background
```

No invisible overlay should block taps/clicks.

# ACCESS KEY GENERATION

After successful creation, automatically generate a unique class key.

Example:

```text
Class Key

DSA7-K29X
```

Provide:

```text
Copy Key
Share Key
Regenerate Key
```

Optionally:

```text
QR Code
```

may be added later if easy and appropriate.

---

# IMPORTANT SECURITY RULE

The class key should behave like an INVITATION CODE.

It must NOT become the permanent authorization mechanism for every request.

Correct:

```text
Student enters key
      ↓
Backend validates key
      ↓
Student membership created
      ↓
Normal authenticated authorization
```

Wrong:

```text
Every file URL contains class key
```

Do NOT expose resources simply because someone knows a predictable URL.

---

# STORE ACCESS KEYS SECURELY

Do NOT store raw access keys unnecessarily if the backend architecture supports secure hashing.

Prefer:

```text
generated key
   ↓
hash
   ↓
database
```

Validate submitted keys against the stored representation.

Never expose internal database IDs as access keys.

---

# ACCESS KEY REQUIREMENTS

Keys should be:

```text
unique
non-sequential
not easily guessable
case-insensitive if practical
easy to type on mobile
```

Avoid confusing characters if desired:

```text
0 / O
1 / I
```

---

# KEY MANAGEMENT

Teacher/class owner should be able to:

```text
View/share current key
Regenerate key
Disable joining
Enable joining
```

When a key is regenerated:

```text
old key
→ becomes invalid

existing members
→ remain members
```

Very important.

Do NOT remove existing students when rotating the class key.

---

# OPTIONAL KEY CONTROLS

If practical, architecture may support:

```text
Expiry date
Maximum joins
Manual approval
```

but these are secondary.

First implement the reliable core system.

# INVALID KEY STATES

Handle:

```text

Expired Key
Joining Disabled
Already Joined
Class Deleted
```

with proper UI messages.

Do not expose sensitive backend information.

---

# RATE LIMIT KEY ATTEMPTS

Protect the join endpoint against brute-force attempts.

Implement reasonable:

```text
rate limiting
validation
logging without secrets
```

Do not reveal whether nearby/partial keys are correct.

# CLASS ROLES

```text
MODERATOR
```

but do not over-engineer the first version.

```text
remove resources
manage members
regenerate access key
disable joining
remove students
```

# AUTHORIZATION MUST BE SERVER-SIDE

This is critical.

Do NOT rely only on frontend hiding.

Every protected class API/resource request must verify:

```text
Authenticated User
        ↓
Class Membership
        ↓
Role / Permission
        ↓
Allow or Deny
```

A user changing a URL manually must NOT gain access to another class.

---

# IDOR PROTECTION

Protect against insecure direct object references.

For routes conceptually like:

```text
/classes/:classId/resources/:resourceId
```

always verify:

```text
resource belongs to class
AND
user belongs to class
```

Do not trust IDs from the browser.

### Multi-Teacher "My Class" System & Dual-Repository Deployment Complete

---

### Key Accomplishments

#### 1. Backend Architecture & Security
- **Multi-Teacher Workspaces**: Any authenticated user can create, manage, and teach their own classrooms with complete privacy and IDOR protection.
- **Invitation Key Protocol**:
  - Secure formatted key generation (e.g. `DSA-7K4P` using unambiguous safe characters).
  - Secure storage using salted hashing (`access_key_hash`).
  - Key rotation (regenerating a key immediately revokes previous keys for new joiners while keeping existing enrolled students).
  - Sliding-window rate limiting on classroom join attempts.
- **Study Notes, Code Resources & Announcements**:
  - Full CRUD with permissions: announcements (with pinning), study notes (with external document/drive links), and code resources.
  - Automatic SQLite schema migration helper (`sync_schema_columns`) executed at startup to preserve existing database records.

#### 2. Mobile-First Red-Black Glassmorphism Frontend
- **Dual-Tab Segregation in `/my-class`**:

  - **"Practice in Compiler"** action on code snippets and assignments that preloads code and language directly into the online compiler playground (`/playground`).

### ROOT CAUSE

**Actual Cause:**
`VERCEL ENV MISMATCH` & `API RESTRICTION / UNPROVISIONED IDENTITY TOOLKIT CONFIG`

**Detailed Explanation:**
1. In the previous deployment, the Vercel production build ran before the newly generated `VITE_FIREBASE_*` environment variables were injected into the Vercel Production scope. As a result, the frontend initialized with a placeholder API key string (`AIzaSy_CODEVAULT_PLACEHOLDER_KEY`), causing the runtime exception `auth/api-key-not-valid`.
2. Furthermore, in newly created Google Cloud/Firebase projects, the underlying Identity Toolkit service (`identitytoolkit.googleapis.com`) required explicit enablement and default Firebase Authentication initialization to recognize client API key requests.
3. The Firebase client service (`frontend/src/services/firebase.ts`) and Login page (`frontend/src/pages/LoginPage.tsx`) were refactored to include singleton validation, secure defaults matching `codevault-pro-souvik`, and friendly error mapping.

Create/reuse a protected endpoint conceptually like:

```text
GET /api/activity
```

The API should:

```text
verify Firebase token
        ↓
extract uid
        ↓
query that UID only
        ↓
return safe sanitized events
```

Do not trust UID from browser query parameters.

depending on the existing routing architecture.

# FIRESTORE RULES

Normal browser users must NOT directly manipulate:

```text
auditLogs
```

Prefer:

```javascript
match /auditLogs/{logId} {
  allow read, create, update, delete: if false;
}
```

when trusted server/Admin SDK is responsible for logs.

Adapt rules appropriately if the existing architecture requires verified client events.

Never allow users to:

```text
delete their history
edit timestamp
change actorUid
change outcome
```

---

# USER HISTORY ACCESS

Normal users may access sanitized own history through a protected server/API layer.

Do not expose direct unrestricted audit collection access.

---

# ADMIN ACCESS

Admin API must independently verify:

```text
Firebase ID token
+
admin custom claim
```

Do not rely only on hiding the Admin button.

---

# INDEXES

Add only indexes needed by real tested queries.

Likely combinations:

```text
createdAt

actorUid + createdAt

category + createdAt

action + createdAt

outcome + createdAt
```

Use:

```text
orderBy(createdAt, "desc")
```

with limits and cursor pagination.

---

# DO NOT LOAD EVERYTHING

Never do:

```text
download entire auditLogs
→ filter in browser
```

Use Firestore query limits and pagination.

---

# RETENTION

Add:

```text
expiresAt
```

if practical.

Recommended initial retention:

```text
90 days
```

Do NOT enable paid TTL/billing features automatically without approval.

If TTL cannot be enabled safely:

keep the field and report the remaining configuration.

---

# FIRESTORE COST CONTROL

Activity logging must not generate massive Firestore usage.

Therefore:

```text
NO every-click tracking

NO page scroll tracking

NO typing tracking

NO animation tracking
```

Only meaningful events.

---

# REQUEST IDs

Generate a:

```text
requestId
```

for important server-side operations.

Use it to associate:

```text
application failure
↔
activity event
```

without exposing internal stack traces.

```text
Firebase ID token verification

request schema validation

allowed action list

payload size limit

rate limiting

sanitized errors

Cache-Control: private, no-store
```

Use same-origin CORS where appropriate.

### Key Accomplishments

1. **Centralized Backend Audit Service (`backend/services/audit_service.py`)**:
   - **Strict Allowlist Enforcement**: Categorizes actions across `auth`, `classroom`, `resource`, `assignment`, `compiler`, `program`, `ai`, `learning`, and `admin`.
   - **Privacy & Security Sanitization**: Automatically strips sensitive fields (passwords, tokens, source code, secrets, API keys, AI prompts) while preserving safe telemetry (language, status, execution duration, verdict, score).
   - **Server-Controlled Timestamps**: Authoritative UTC timestamps presented in Asia/Kolkata (IST).

2. **Secure Activity REST Endpoints (`backend/api/activity.py`)**:
   - `GET /api/activity`: Authenticated user activity history strictly isolated to the requesting user (`actor_uid = current_user.email` or `user_<id>`).
   - `POST /api/activity/log`: Validated client-side activity reporting securely bound to the verified Firebase/JWT token identity.
   - `GET /api/admin/activity` & `GET /api/admin/activity/stats`: Role-protected global audit log query and stats engine accessible only to users with server-verified Administrator privileges.

3. **Firestore Security Rules (`firestore.rules`)**:
   - Updated `firestore.rules` with strict access controls: direct browser creation/editing/deletion of `auditLogs` is denied (`allow create, update, delete: if false`), while read access is restricted to the event owner or an authenticated admin.

4. **Frontend Interfaces**:
   - **`/my-activity` ([`UserActivityPage.tsx`](file:///E:/Code%20Vault%20pro/Code/frontend/src/pages/UserActivityPage.tsx))**: Responsive, mobile-first timeline with category filter pills, time range filtering, outcome badges, and IST date grouping.
   - **`/admin/activity` ([`AdminActivityDashboardPage.tsx`](file:///E:/Code%20Vault%20pro/Code/frontend/src/pages/AdminActivityDashboardPage.tsx))**: Global audit investigation dashboard with metrics banner, full-text search, multi-filter toolbar, paginated table, and metadata inspect modal.
   - **Navigation ([`Navbar.tsx`](file:///E:/Code%20Vault%20pro/Code/frontend/src/components/Navbar.tsx))**: Added direct links to activity history and administrative dashboard for authorized users.

---

### Verification & Deployment

- **Automated Tests**: **22 / 22** pytest test suite passed (including isolation, sanitization, and admin access control).
- **Frontend Build**: Production bundle built cleanly with Vite.
- **GitHub Repository**: Pushed to [`https://github.com/S0u1k/Code-Vault-Pro`](https://github.com/S0u1k/Code-Vault-Pro) (Commit `2a59c8a`).

  - Verified `GET /api/activity` -> HTTP 200 (self-only activity timeline)

# IF USING DIRECT CLIENT FIRESTORE

If the project intentionally uses Firebase client Firestore and it can be secured cleanly:

implement correct rules.

For classroom create, rules must conceptually verify:

```text
request.auth != null

AND

request.resource.data.ownerUid == request.auth.uid
```

Do not copy this blindly if schema differs.

Use the actual Firestore schema.

---

# RECOMMENDED CANONICAL DATA MODEL

Adapt to existing naming conventions, but converge on ONE structure.

## Classroom

```text
classrooms/{classroomId}
```

Conceptually:

```typescript
{
  name: string,
  subject: string,
  section: string | null,
  description: string | null,

  ownerUid: string,

  accessKeyNormalized: string,

  joiningEnabled: boolean,

  createdAt: serverTimestamp(),
  updatedAt: serverTimestamp()
}
```

---

# OWNER MEMBERSHIP

Automatically create:

```text
classrooms/{classroomId}/members/{ownerUid}
```

with:

```typescript
{
  uid: ownerUid,
  role: "owner",
  joinedAt: serverTimestamp()
}
```

The creator MUST NOT need to join their own class.

---

# USER-SIDE CLASS INDEX

For reliable My Class/Profile querying, use:

```text
users/{uid}/classrooms/{classroomId}
```

Conceptually:

```typescript
{
  classroomId: string,
  role: "owner" | "student",
  joinedAt: serverTimestamp()
}
```

This makes:

```text
Profile → My Classrooms

My Class → Classrooms
```

fast and deterministic.

---

# BIDIRECTIONAL MEMBERSHIP

Maintain both:

```text
classrooms/{classroomId}/members/{uid}
```

and:

```text
users/{uid}/classrooms/{classroomId}
```

in the SAME trusted transaction/batch.

This allows:

```text
Classroom → members
User → classrooms
```

without expensive scanning.

---

# ACCESS KEY LOOKUP

Do NOT query every classroom publicly just to find a key.

Recommended secure mapping:

```text
classAccessKeys/{normalizedKey}
```

containing:

```typescript
{
  classroomId: string,
  createdAt: serverTimestamp()
}
```

Do NOT place private classroom data in this lookup.

---

# CLASS KEY NORMALIZATION

Create one canonical function used by BOTH creation and joining.

Conceptually:

```typescript
normalizeClassKey(key) =
  key
    .trim()
    .toUpperCase()
    .replace(/\s+/g, "")
```

Examples:

```text
abc-k7p4
ABC-K7P4
 ABC-K7P4
```

should resolve consistently.

---

# CLASS KEY UNIQUENESS

Before finalizing a new classroom key:

verify the normalized key is not already assigned.

If collision:

```text
generate another key
```

Do NOT overwrite another classroom's lookup.

---

# CLASSROOM CREATE — ATOMIC TRANSACTION

Preferred create operation:

```text
Generate Firestore classroom ID
        ↓
Generate unique key
        ↓
Trusted transaction/batch
```

Write:

```text
1. classrooms/{classroomId}

2. classrooms/{classroomId}/members/{ownerUid}

3. users/{ownerUid}/classrooms/{classroomId}

4. classAccessKeys/{normalizedKey}
```

Commit ONCE.

The UI must NOT show:

```text
Class Created Successfully
```

until the complete transaction succeeds.

If owner membership fails:

class creation is NOT complete.

If class-key lookup fails:

class creation is NOT complete.

Do not create partial classroom state.

```text
1 click
=
1 request
```

while request is in progress.

Frontend authenticated API client should obtain:

```text
currentUser.getIdToken()
```

and send:

```text
Authorization: Bearer <Firebase ID Token>
```

Never print the token.

Never put the token in:

```text
URL
query string
GitHub
logs
```

---

# SERVER TOKEN VERIFICATION

Backend must verify the Firebase ID token using the secure existing Firebase Admin infrastructure.

Concept:

```text
Bearer token
      ↓
verifyIdToken()
      ↓
decoded.uid
      ↓
authorized operation
```

Do NOT trust UID from request body.

```text
frontend source
public/
GitHub
Firestore documents
```

# CodeVault Pro — Complete Online AI + Offline Study System Report

---

## 1. Summary of Accomplishments

1. **Online-Only CodeVault AI**:
   - Completely eliminated the old offline local LLM / browser model runtime (removed WebGPU capability detectors, model downloading, model caching, and offline Web Workers).
   - CodeVault AI is strictly an online cloud service powered by serverless endpoints (`/api/ai/chat`, `/api/ai/explain`, `/api/ai/suggest-fix`, `/api/ai/health`).
   - If the user loses internet access, the chat panel provides a clean notification: *"AI temporarily unavailable — check your connection"*, without attempting local browser inference.

2. **Clean Branding & Provider Neutrality**:
   - Product name is uniformly **CodeVault AI**.
   - Removed all provider and model names (`NVIDIA`, `Nemotron`, `NVIDIA Nemotron`) from user-facing badges, headers, messages, and disclaimer strings.
   - Upstream API keys remain securely confined to server-side environment variables and are never leaked to frontend code or client bundles.

3. **Liquid Glass Backdrop & Long-Chat Scroll Bug Fixed**:
   - **Fixed Backdrop**: The decorative glass mesh, radial gradient, and ambient glow backdrop are pinned with `position: absolute; inset: 0; pointer-events: none;` inside the chat shell. The background remains stable during chat scrolling.
   - **Independent Scrolling**: The message container (`flex: 1; overflow-y: auto;`) scrolls independently.
   - **Smart Auto-Scroll**: Messages automatically scroll down when already near the bottom. If the user scrolls up to review previous messages, the view stays in place and presents a floating `↓ Latest` button to jump to the newest response.
   - **Code Blocks**: Formatted in syntax-highlighted dark containers with an independent horizontal scroll bar and a one-click copy button.
   - **Mobile Viewport**: Fully responsive 100dvh layout suitable for 360px–430px mobile screens without horizontal page overflow.

4. **IndexedDB Offline Study Materials System**:
   - Implemented [`studyMaterialsStorage.ts`](file:///E:/Code%20Vault%20pro/Code/frontend/src/services/studyMaterialsStorage.ts) (`codevault-study-materials-v1` database) to cache DSA lessons, lecture notes, PDFs, and shared code snippets for offline reading.
   - Built a reusable [`OfflineDownloadButton`](file:///E:/Code%20Vault%20pro/Code/frontend/src/components/OfflineDownloadButton.tsx) component indicating offline status (`Download Offline` / `Saved Offline ✓`).
   - Added a dedicated **Downloads** tab in [`MyClassPage.tsx`](file:///E:/Code%20Vault%20pro/Code/frontend/src/pages/MyClassPage.tsx) powered by [`OfflineDownloadsTab.tsx`](file:///E:/Code%20Vault%20pro/Code/frontend/src/components/OfflineDownloadsTab.tsx) to browse, view, and manage locally saved study documents.
   - Added user UID data isolation in [`AuthContext.tsx`](file:///E:/Code%20Vault%20pro/Code/frontend/src/context/AuthContext.tsx) to clear private classroom materials upon logout.

5. **Service Worker v3.0.0**:
   - Updated [`public/sw.js`](file:///E:/Code%20Vault%20pro/Code/frontend/public/sw.js) to cache only static assets and the single-page application shell, completely removing model weight caching logic.

# OBSERVED BUG FROM THE VIDEO

On mobile, when a modal such as:

```text
Create Classroom
```

is open and the user scrolls:

* the page BEHIND the modal continues scrolling
* the red / liquid-glass background moves with the document
* the footer moves upward and appears through/over the modal
* fixed/mobile navigation can overlap the modal
* the modal does not behave like a true viewport-level overlay
* the background and modal appear to belong to the same scrolling document
* the UI visually jumps as scrolling continues
* scrolling feels like multiple layers are moving independently

This is especially visible on mobile Chrome.

Fix this at the ROOT architecture level.

---

# EXPECTED RESULT

When ANY modal/dialog/bottom sheet is open:

```text
BACKGROUND PAGE
→ LOCKED

AMBIENT RED/BLACK BACKGROUND
→ STABLE

FOOTER
→ STAYS BEHIND MODAL

BOTTOM NAV
→ STAYS BEHIND MODAL

MODAL BACKDROP
→ FIXED TO VIEWPORT

MODAL PANEL
→ ONLY MODAL CONTENT MAY SCROLL

PAGE BEHIND
→ MUST NOT SCROLL
```

Closing the modal must restore the page to the EXACT scroll position it had before the modal opened.


---

# PREFERRED MODAL ARCHITECTURE

The modal should exist at viewport level.

In React, strongly prefer using a Portal:

```text
ReactDOM.createPortal(...)
        ↓
document.body
```

or the project's existing proper Dialog/Portal system.

Conceptually:

```text
document.body

├── App
│   ├── Background
│   ├── Header
│   ├── Page Content
│   ├── Footer
│   └── Bottom Navigation
│
└── Modal Portal
    ├── Fixed Backdrop
    └── Modal Panel
```

This prevents page transforms/stacking contexts from interfering with the modal.

---

# MODAL OVERLAY

The overlay must cover the REAL viewport.

Conceptually:

```css
position: fixed;
inset: 0;
width: 100%;
height: 100dvh;
```

Use appropriate safe-area handling.

Do not use document-height positioning for the modal.

# PART 2 — BODY SCROLL LOCK

When a modal opens:

LOCK background/document scrolling.

```text

```

or use the project's existing accessible Dialog library if available.

Requirements:

```text
modal opens
→ record current page scroll position
→ lock page scroll

modal closes
→ unlock page
→ restore exact previous scroll position
```

```text
lost scroll position
permanent body lock
```

# PART 3 — MODAL INTERNAL SCROLL

The modal may contain a long form.

For example:

```text
Create Classroom

Class Name
Subject
Section
Description
...
Create & Generate Key
```

On a small phone, the form may exceed viewport height.

Correct architecture:

```text
Fixed Modal Overlay
        ↓
Modal Container
        ↓
Scrollable Modal Body
```

ONLY the modal body should scroll.

---

# MODAL HEIGHT

Use mobile-safe sizing.

Conceptually:

```css
max-height:
calc(
  100dvh
  - env(safe-area-inset-top)
  - env(safe-area-inset-bottom)
  - spacing
);
```

Do not rely only on:

```css
100vh
```

because mobile browser chrome can change the visible viewport.

Prefer:

```text
dvh
svh
```

where appropriate.

---

# MODAL SCROLL CONTAINMENT

Use appropriate:

```css
overflow-y: auto;
overscroll-behavior: contain;
-webkit-overflow-scrolling: touch;
```

on the actual modal scroll container.

Prevent scroll chaining from the modal into the page behind it.

---

When user swipes the backdrop:

```text

```

preserve that behavior.

# PART 4 — FIX GLOBAL RED / LIQUID BACKGROUND

The animated red-black atmospheric background should NOT be tied to page content positions.

It should visually behave like a stable ambient layer behind the application.

Preferred architecture:

```text
Viewport
│
├── Fixed Ambient Background
│
└── Scrollable Application Content
```

---

# AMBIENT BACKGROUND

Use one stable global background layer.

Conceptually:

```css
position: fixed;
inset: 0;
z-index: 0;
pointer-events: none;
overflow: hidden;
```

Application content renders above it.

---

# DO NOT USE MOBILE `background-attachment: fixed` AS THE MAIN SOLUTION

Mobile browsers handle:

```css
background-attachment: fixed;
```

inconsistently.

Instead prefer a dedicated:

```text
position: fixed
```

background element/pseudo-element.

### 🤖 Assistant Response

I have updated the modal architecture across the application and am waiting for the production build verification.

ROOT CAUSE:
1. Modals were rendered in-line inside the page DOM tree (`<main>`) rather than a React Portal at `document.body`, making their viewport positioning subject to parent scroll offsets, layout shifts, and containing stacking contexts.
2. Z-index hierarchy was fragmented (`z-50` was concurrently assigned to modals, Navbar, MobileBottomNav, and drawer elements), causing the fixed footer (`z-10`) and navigation bars to interleave and overlap the modal during document scrolling.
3. Absence of a body scroll locking mechanism allowed touch gestures and scroll wheel events to bubble into the background document, moving the underlying page while the modal was open.
4. Ambient background element was rendered with a relative stacking context that moved and jittered during mobile document scroll instead of remaining firmly anchored at the root viewport level (`position: fixed; inset: 0; pointer-events: none; z-index: 0;`).

# CURRENT BUG

The current Classroom behavior is approximately:

```text
Login
→ My Class
→ Create Classroom
→ Form submitted
→ A Class Key is generated
```

BUT then:

```text
Classroom card does not reliably appear ❌

Created classroom is not immediately previewed ❌

Page/query does not refresh correctly ❌

Refresh may lose/not show the classroom ❌

Classroom cannot be opened ❌

Direct classroom page may show:
"Classroom not found or access denied" ❌

Another user entering the generated key
may get Invalid / Not Found ❌
```

This means the application may currently be treating:

```text
Key generated
```

as equivalent to:

```text
Classroom fully created
```

even though the persistent database/membership/UI state is incomplete.

FIX THIS ARCHITECTURALLY.

---

# ABSOLUTE SUCCESS FLOW

After the repair the OWNER flow must be:

```text
Firebase Login
      ↓
Create Classroom
      ↓
Server/Firestore transaction succeeds
      ↓
Classroom document exists
      ↓
Owner membership exists
      ↓
User → Classroom index exists
      ↓
Class Key lookup exists
      ↓
UI receives SUCCESS
      ↓
Create modal closes
      ↓
New Classroom card appears IMMEDIATELY
      ↓
Owner clicks Classroom
      ↓
Classroom opens successfully
```

Refreshing must preserve everything.

### 🤖 Assistant Response

ROOT CAUSE:
1. In `ClassroomDetailPage`, subcollections (`announcements`, `resources`, `assignments`, `members`) were loaded via an all-or-nothing `Promise.all` before Firebase auth initialization completed (`authLoading`), causing permission evaluation failures to completely reject the query and falsely display "Classroom not found or access denied".
2. In `firestore.rules`, `isClassOwner()` accessed `get().data.owner_id` without checking `exists()`, causing evaluation crashes on uncommitted or nonexistent documents.
3. In `MyClassPage` and `CreatorPage`, classroom state relied on single-shot queries without real-time listeners (`onSnapshot`), and modal close handlers did not reset `createdClassInfo` upon dismissal, preventing clean re-creation and instant reactive list updates.

### 🤖 Assistant Response

### Verification & System Status

All core components and fixes have been successfully implemented, tested, pushed, and verified live on production:

1. **Atomic Classroom Persistence & Key Management**:
   - Atomic Firestore batch writes for Classroom creation (`classrooms/{classroomId}`), owner membership (`classrooms/{classroomId}/members/{ownerUid}`), user-side index (`users/{ownerUid}/classrooms/{classroomId}`), and O(1) access key registry (`classAccessKeys/{normalizedKey}`).
   - Safe `exists()` checks in `firestore.rules` preventing null evaluation errors.

2. **Real-time Lifecycle & Instant Refresh**:
   - `useUserClassrooms` hook provides real-time Firestore listeners (`onSnapshot`) with optimistic instant preview upon classroom creation and join.
   - Clean dismissal and state resets for Create/Join modals so subsequent actions always open with fresh forms.

3. **Workspace Loading & Error Resilience**:
   - `ClassroomDetailPage` safely waits for auth restoration (`authLoading`), loads the main classroom document first, and fetches subcollections with `Promise.allSettled` to prevent missing/empty collections from crashing the page.
   - Interactive on-page access key enrollment allows students opening `/classrooms/:id` to join directly.

4. **Production Deployment & Repository Status**:
   - **GitHub Primary (`origin/main`)**: Synced and up to date (Commit `dcdd66d`).
   - **Vercel Production**: Live and active at [`https://codevault-pro-weld.vercel.app`](https://codevault-pro-weld.vercel.app).
   - **Backend & Frontend Tests**: 100% passing.


### 🤖 Assistant Response

আপনার ব্যাকগ্রাউন্ডে থাকা **৩টি টাস্কই সম্পূর্ণ করে এখন বন্ধ (Terminate/Kill)** করে দেওয়া হয়েছে। বর্তমানে কোনো ব্যাকগ্রাউন্ড টাস্ক চালু নেই।

---

### এই ৩টি টাস্ক কী কাজের জন্য প্রয়োজন হয়েছিল:

1. **টাস্ক ১ (`Task 3778` — Test batch classroom creation)**:
   - **উদ্দেশ্য**: Firebase Firestore-এ যখন একজন শিক্ষক/ওনার নতুন ক্লাসরুম তৈরি করেন, তখন ক্লাসরুম ডকুমেন্ট (`classrooms`), ওনার মেম্বারশিপ (`members`), ইউজারের ইনডেক্স (`users/{uid}/classrooms`), এবং ফাস্ট অ্যাক্সেস কি রেজিস্ট্রি (`classAccessKeys`) যাতে একবারে **অ্যাটমিক ব্যাচে (Atomic Batch Write)** সফলভাবে সংরক্ষিত হয়, তা নোড স্ক্রিপ্ট দিয়ে লাইভ টেস্ট করা হয়েছিল।

2. **টাস্ক ২ (`Task 3782` — Run full test suite)**:
   - **উদ্দেশ্য**: দুজন পৃথক অথেনটিকেটেড ইউজার নিয়ে এন্ড-টু-এন্ড রিয়েল ক্লাসরুম ফ্লো টেস্ট করা হয়েছিল:
     - ইউজার ১ (ওনার): ক্লাসরুম তৈরি করে কি পেয়েছেন।
     - ইউজার ২ (স্টুডেন্ট): সেই কি দিয়ে সরাসরি জয়েন করেছেন।
     - জয়েন করার পর দুজনের ড্যাশবোর্ডেই ক্লাসরুম, অ্যানাউন্সমেন্ট এবং মেম্বার লিস্ট কোনো রিফ্রেশ ছাড়াই রিয়েল-টাইমে আপডেট হচ্ছে কিনা তা নিশ্চিত করা হয়েছিল।

3. **টাস্ক ৩ (`Task 3912` — Owner cleanup test)**:
   - **উদ্দেশ্য**: টেস্টিংয়ের জন্য ডেটাবেসে তৈরি করা সাময়িক টেস্ট রেকর্ডগুলো ওনার অ্যাকাউন্টের সিকিউরিটি রুলস যাচাই করে নিরাপদে সম্পূর্ণ ক্লিনআপ/ডিলিট করা হয়েছিল, যাতে লাইভ ডেটাবেসে কোনো অপ্রয়োজনীয় ডামি ডেটা না থাকে।

---

### বর্তমান স্ট্যাটাস:
- **সবকটি টাস্কের কাজ সফলভাবে সমাপ্ত হয়েছে** এবং তিনটি ব্যাকগ্রাউন্ড প্রসেসই বন্ধ করে দেওয়া হয়েছে।
- প্রোডাকশন সাইট ([`https://codevault-pro-weld.vercel.app`](https://codevault-pro-weld.vercel.app)) সম্পূর্ণ সক্রিয় ও ত্রুটিমুক্ত রয়েছে।

### 🤖 Assistant Response

ROOT CAUSE OF WHITE SCREEN:
1. `index.html` lacked an inline dark background on `<html>`, `<body>`, and `#root`, relying entirely on external Tailwind CSS which caused browsers to paint a default white screen during initial network arrival.
2. `#root` was completely empty before React mount, showing nothing until the JavaScript bundle finished downloading, parsing, and hydrating.
3. Heavy sub-pages (Monaco compiler, Recharts dashboards, classroom workspaces) were imported synchronously at the root of `App.tsx`, causing a heavy 518 kB initial JavaScript bundle on first paint.
4. AI health checks were firing immediately in constructors on page load, contending for early network bandwidth.

RAW HTML INITIAL BACKGROUND:
PASS

INITIAL APP SHELL:
PASS

REACT MOUNT:
PASS

AUTH LOADING:
PASS

FIREBASE STARTUP:
PASS

GLOBAL `return null` STATES:
FIXED

PASS

111.82 kB (29.11 kB gzipped) — 78% reduction from 518 kB

LARGEST INITIAL CHUNKS:

# CodeVault Pro — Classroom Study Library Integration Report

## 1. Accomplishments Summary

### 📚 Structured Classroom Study Library Architecture
- Created [`studyLibraryRegistry.ts`](file:///E:/Code%20Vault%20pro/Code/frontend/src/services/studyLibraryRegistry.ts) as the single source of truth for curated academic curricula:
  - **🧠 DSA (Data Structures & Algorithms)**: Kept as an independent core track covering Sorting (Bubble, Selection, Insertion), Searching (Binary, Linear), Linked Lists (Singly, Doubly, Reversal), Stacks & Queues, Trees, Graphs, and Competitive Programming problem sets.
    - Multi-language implementations: **C**, **Python**, and **Java** with full syntax-highlighted code.
    - **"Practice in Compiler"** action: Automatically redirects to `/playground` with the code and language prefilled.
    - Direct verified links to **GitHub repositories** and **Mega document drives**.
  - **💻 C Programming**: Separated top-level category featuring Fundamentals, Syntax, Memory Allocation & Pointers (`malloc`/`free`), Handwritten notes, and Practice assignments.
  - **🐍 Python Programming**: Python Syntax, OOP Architecture, Curated Assignment sheets, and GitHub code vaults.
  - **☕ Java Programming**: OOP principles, Theory notes, and Curated assignments.
- Built [`ClassroomStudyLibrary.tsx`](file:///E:/Code%20Vault%20pro/Code/frontend/src/components/classroom/ClassroomStudyLibrary.tsx) with:
  - Subject switcher cards (DSA, C, Python, Java) with resource counts and color badges.
  - Dynamic DSA language track filter (`All`, `C`, `Python`, `Java`, `C++`).
  - Real-time search across topics, algorithm titles, languages, and descriptions.
  - Interactive compiler handoff via `sessionStorage` prefill to `/playground`.
  - Offline local material downloads via IndexedDB [`studyMaterialsStorage.ts`](file:///E:/Code%20Vault%20pro/Code/frontend/src/services/studyMaterialsStorage.ts).
  - Teacher/Owner resource creation modal for adding custom classroom theory, code, assignments, and storage links.

### 🛡️ Firestore Sync & Security Rules
- Implemented [`studyLibraryFirestore.ts`](file:///E:/Code%20Vault%20pro/Code/frontend/src/services/studyLibraryFirestore.ts) for real-time Firestore subscriptions on `classrooms/{classId}/library`.
- Updated [`firestore.rules`](file:///E:/Code%20Vault%20pro/Code/firestore.rules) to enforce secure access:
  - `match /library/{resourceId}`: members read, owner manages and deletes.

### 🏫 Target Classroom Integration & Preservation of Creator Page
- Integrated the **Study Library** tab into [`ClassroomDetailPage.tsx`](file:///E:/Code%20Vault%20pro/Code/frontend/src/pages/ClassroomDetailPage.tsx) with real-time sync.
- Target classroom resolved: `GlggTqbynPuuAbmSgmhv` (*"Pranab sir (Study Material)"*, Key: `CSE-Q7Z8H`).
- Preserved `/creator` route and [`CreatorPage.tsx`](file:///E:/Code%20Vault%20pro/Code/frontend/src/pages/CreatorPage.tsx) completely active and intact.
